### [74] [Grounding Text Embeddings in Stakeholder Associations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27168)
*Jonathan Rystrøm,Sofie Burgos-Thorsen,Zihao Fu,Johan Irving Søltoft,Kenneth C. Enevoldsen,Chris Russell*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为“Stakeholder Grounding Exercise”的方法，用于评估文本嵌入模型是否与人类专家的语义判断对齐。以丹麦政策问题和美国AI用例两项研究为例，发现神经文本嵌入模型与人类专家之间存在显著差距（16-26个百分点），且这种不对齐会传播到下游聚类任务中。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 文本嵌入被广泛用于分析复杂文本语料，但不清楚嵌入模型是否捕捉到了与人类专家相同的语义距离。确保嵌入表示与人类意图对齐，对于保证分析的有效性至关重要。

Method: 提出Stakeholder Grounding Exercise方法，通过让专家明确表达关联性，将嵌入模型的结果锚定在人类理解上。在丹麦政策问题（主要研究）和美国联邦AI用例（复制研究）两个案例中，比较神经文本嵌入与人类专家的语义判断一致性。

Result: 神经文本嵌入的可靠性比人类专家低19-26个百分点；这种不对齐会传播到下游聚类性能（Spearman ρ=0.9）；在英语环境下使用数字协议和不同专家社区的复制研究中，同样存在16个百分点的差距。

Conclusion: Stakeholder Grounding Exercise提供了一种实用方法，用于评估嵌入模型是否捕捉到了对领域专家最重要的语义区分。研究表明嵌入模型与人类专家之间存在系统性的对齐差距，且该差距不是单一工具或领域的人工产物。

Abstract: Text embeddings are widely used to analyse large corpora of complex texts. However, it is unclear whether the embeddings capture the same semantic distances as the human experts using them. Ensuring alignment between embedding representations and human intentions is essential for valid analyses. We present the Stakeholder Grounding Exercise, a method for making expert associations explicit and grounding embedding model results in human understanding. In our primary case study on Danish policy issues, we find that neural text embeddings are substantially less reliable than human experts (19-26 pp gap), and that this misalignment propagates to downstream clustering performance (Spearman $ρ=0.9$ between exercise ranking and cluster quality). A secondary study on US Federal AI use cases replicates the gap (16pp) in English, using a digital protocol and a different community of experts -- demonstrating that the gap is not an artefact of a single instrument or domain. The Stakeholder Grounding Exercise offers a practical method for assessing whether embedding models capture the semantic distinctions that matter most to domain experts.

</details>


### [75] [MAIGO: Mitigating Lost-in-Conversation with History-Cleaned On-Policy Self-Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27186)
*Haoyu Zheng,Yun Zhu,Shu Yuan,Shangming Chen,Qing Wang,Wenqiao Zhang,Jun Xiao,Yueting Zhuang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 大型语言模型在长对话中性能下降（lost-in-conversation gap），部分原因是中间助手回复的自我污染。本文提出MAIGO，一种基于策略的自我蒸馏方法，通过清理历史参考来减少这种污染，在不使用验证器或推理时框架的情况下，将SHARDED准确率从52.8提升至66.1，SHARDED/FULL比率从66.5%提升至84.1%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型在完全指定提示下能很好完成任务，但在多轮对话中性能下降（LiC gap）。作者追溯部分退化到自我污染机制：中间助手回复进入后续上下文，将早期偏差向前传播。

Method: 提出MAIGO，一种on-policy自我蒸馏方法。(1) 对中间轮次，移除先前助手回复同时保留用户可见的分片前缀；(2) 对回答轮次，从完整的全视角参考中蒸馏；(3) 使用可靠性权重降低与干净参考不一致的中间轮次样本权重。无需验证器奖励、状态标签或推理时框架。

Result: 在LiC配对视图协议和确定性验证器下，MAIGO将Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct的SHARDED准确率从52.8提升至66.1，SHARDED/FULL比率从66.5%提升至84.1%，同时FULL准确率下降在2.3点以内。

Conclusion: 自我污染是LiC gap中一个可训练的成分，MAIGO通过自我蒸馏有效缓解了这一问题。

Abstract: Large language models often solve tasks from a fully specified prompt but degrade when the same requirements unfold over multiple turns, known as the lost-in-conversation (LiC) gap. We trace part of this degradation to self-contamination: intermediate assistant replies enter later context and carry early deviations forward. Motivated by this mechanism, we propose MAIGO, an on-policy self-distillation method that reduces this contamination using history-cleaned references from the model's own policy. For middle turns, MAIGO removes prior assistant replies while preserving the user-visible sharded prefix; for answer turns, it distills from paired full-view references conditioned on the completed user-side dialogue. A reliability weight downweights middle-turn samples that disagree with the clean reference. MAIGO requires no verifier rewards, state labels, or inference-time scaffolding. Under the LiC paired-view protocol with deterministic verifiers, MAIGO improves Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct SHARDED accuracy from 52.8 to 66.1 and the SHARDED/FULL ratio from 66.5% to 84.1%, while keeping FULL accuracy within 2.3 points. These results show that self-contamination is a trainable component of the LiC gap.

</details>


### [76] [Beyond Binary: Speech Representations Across the Cognitive Score Hierarchy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27189)
*Serli Kopar,Roshan Prakash Rane,Christian Mychajliw,Lydia Federmann,Gerhard Eschweiler,Daniela Berg,Sam Gijsen,Paula Andrea Perez-Toro,Kerstin Ritter*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本研究利用5,754份德语神经心理学评估录音，分析了轻度认知障碍中语音表征与认知评估层级结构的关系。结果显示，自监督学习嵌入在低层级任务中优于手工声学特征，但在MCI分类中趋势反转；任务约束影响层级性能，自由度高的任务呈现“专家型”表征，结构化任务呈现“通才型”表征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究语音表征与认知评估层级结构之间的关系，明确不同任务约束如何影响从低级声学特征到高级认知状态的分类性能，从而理解自动化临床语音分析中的任务与评估层级间的联系。

Method: 使用5,754份德语神经心理学评估录音，评估六项认知任务在三个分数层级（任务级、领域级、全局级）的表现，比较手工声学特征与自监督学习（SSL）嵌入向量的分类性能。

Result: SSL表征在低层级任务中优于手工特征，但在MCI分类中手工特征表现更好；自由度高的任务随层级升高性能稀释，呈现“专家型”表征；高度结构化任务随层级升高性能提升，呈现“通才型”表征。

Conclusion: 任务约束与评估层级之间存在系统性关联，不同任务类型在层级化临床语音分析中表现出不同的表征特性，这为自动化认知评估提供了重要指导。

Abstract: This study examines the relationship between speech representations and the hierarchical structure of cognitive assessment in mild cognitive impairment. Utilizing 5,754 German neuropsychological assessment recordings, we evaluate six cognitive tasks across three score levels: task, domain, and global levels. We compare hand-crafted acoustic features with self-supervised learning (SSL) embeddings. Results show that although SSL representations generally outperform hand-crafted features at lower levels, this trend reverses for MCI classification. Furthermore, task-specific constraints influence performance: tasks with greater response freedom exhibit performance dilution as hierarchical levels increase, suggesting ``specialist'' representations, whereas the performance of highly structured tasks increases toward higher levels, suggesting ``generalist'' representations. These findings show links between task constraints and assessment hierarchy in automated clinical speech analysis.

</details>


### [77] [Learning When to Think While Listening in Large Audio-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27190)
*Zhiyuan Song,Weici Zhao,Yang Xiao,Suhao Yu,Cheng Zhu,Jiatao Gu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种面向大型音频语言模型（LALM）的可学习"等待-思考-回答"控制框架。该控制器在部分音频证据下动态决策何时等待、何时输出中间推理更新、何时给出最终答案。基于Qwen2.5-Omni-7B模型，通过监督微调和六奖励策略优化（DAPO）训练，在合成基准上将行加权准确率从67.6%提升至70.3%，并将终点后最终思考长度减少14%，在真实音频基准上同样有效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在流式语音交互场景中，推理质量与响应速度之间存在根本性矛盾：过早回答可能基于不完整证据，过晚回答则导致用户可见延迟。现有LALM缺乏在部分音频输入下动态控制推理时机的能力，亟需一种能决定何时等待、何时输出中间推理、何时给出最终答案的可学习机制。

Method: 提出wait-think-answer控制框架，基于Qwen2.5-Omni-7B构建对齐的推理轨迹，依次执行SFT和六奖励DAPO策略优化。奖励函数综合考虑答案正确性、动作有效性、更新时机、延迟同步、推理质量和链一致性，优化整个轨迹而非仅最终答案。

Result: 在六任务合成SRQA基准上，六奖励DAPO控制器将行加权准确率从67.6%提升至70.3%，终点后最终思考长度减少14%。在186样本真实音频基准上，SFT取得最强准确率，六奖励DAPO是唯一最终思考长度低于基线的学习变体。

Conclusion: 流式语音模型应当学习在音频流中何时显式化中间推理过程。可学习的wait-think-answer控制机制能有效平衡推理质量与响应延迟，在合成和真实语音场景下均表现良好。

Abstract: Recent advances in Large Audio-Language Models (LALMs) have made real-time, streaming spoken interaction increasingly practical. In this setting, reasoning quality and responsiveness are tightly coupled: delaying reasoning until the speech endpoint can improve answer quality but moves deliberation into user-visible response delay, while answering too early risks committing before decisive evidence arrives. We introduce a learnable wait-think-answer control formulation for LALMs. Motivated by the incremental nature of human conversation, the controller decides under partial audio evidence when to wait, when to externalize a compact reasoning update, and when to answer. Using Qwen2.5-Omni-7B as the base model, we construct aligned wait-think-answer traces from spoken reasoning data, train the controller with supervised fine-tuning (SFT), and then apply Decoupled Clip and Dynamic Sampling Policy Optimization (DAPO). The reward combines answer correctness, action validity, update timing, latency synchronization, reasoning quality, and chain consistency, optimizing the complete wait-think-answer trajectory and not the final answer alone. On a six-task synthetic spoken reasoning question answering (SRQA) benchmark, the six-reward DAPO controller improves the row-weighted accuracy from 67.6% to 70.3% while reducing post-endpoint final-think length by 14% under the same Qwen deployment harness. On a 186-item human-recorded Real Audio Bench, a transfer check beyond text-to-speech (TTS)-rendered speech, the controller family remains functional: SFT achieves the strongest accuracy, while the six-reward DAPO controller is the only learned variant whose final-think length falls below the base. These results suggest that a streaming model should learn when to make intermediate reasoning explicit during the audio stream.

</details>


### [78] [EpiCurveBench: Evaluating VLMs on Epidemic Curve Digitization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27195)
*Thomas Berkane,Maimuna S. Majumder*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出EpiCurveBench（1000张真实流行病曲线图基准）和EpiCurveSimilarity（基于动态规划的时间序列对齐评估指标），解决了现有图表数据提取评测中忽略时间结构、指标过于粗糙的问题。最强模型仅达52.3% ECS，且ECS比传统指标更具区分度，与四项下游流行病学统计量相关性比DTW高1.5-3.6倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有图表数据提取评测（如ChartQA）存在两个缺口：1）前沿VLM已超89%准确率，头部空间不足；2）评估指标将提取点视为无序键值对，忽略时间序列的时间结构，且对微小对齐偏移惩罚过大。需要针对时间序列图表的高质量评测基准和指标。

Method: 1）构建EpiCurveBench：1000张来自多样公共卫生源的实时流行病曲线图；2）提出EpiCurveSimilarity (ECS)：通过动态规划对齐预测序列与真实序列，容忍并比例惩罚局部时间偏移和间隔；3）评估6种方法（3个前沿闭源VLM、1个开源VLM、2个专业图表提取系统）；4）与4项下游流行病学汇总统计量（总计数、峰值时间、峰值幅度、增长率保真度）进行验证。

Result: 最强模型仅达52.3% ECS；ECS将4个通用VLM分散在25个百分点的范围内，而传统键值指标（RMS、SCRM）仅压缩在5个百分点内；ECS与四项下游统计量的相关性比DTW高1.5-3.6倍。

Conclusion: EpiCurveBench和ECS可推广到任何结构化时间序列图表提取场景，为解锁被困在已发表图表中的疫情数据提供高影响公共卫生应用。

Abstract: Chart-to-data extraction with vision-language models (VLMs) is increasingly evaluated on benchmarks that show diminishing headroom (frontier VLMs exceed 89% on ChartQA) and with metrics that treat extracted points as unordered key-value pairs, ignoring the temporal structure of time series and penalizing small alignment shifts as catastrophic failures. We address both gaps with EpiCurveBench, a benchmark of 1,000 real-world epidemic curve images curated from diverse public-health sources, and EpiCurveSimilarity (ECS), an evaluation metric that aligns predicted and ground-truth series via dynamic programming, tolerating local temporal shifts and gaps while penalizing them proportionally. Evaluating six methods--three frontier closed VLMs, one open VLM, and two specialized chart-extraction systems--we find the strongest model reaches only 52.3% ECS, and that ECS spreads the four general-purpose VLMs over a 25-point range where key-value metrics (RMS, SCRM) compress them into a 5-point band. We further validate ECS against four downstream epidemiological summary statistics, finding that higher ECS predicts smaller errors in total counts, peak timing, and peak magnitude, and higher growth-rate fidelity; across all four, ECS correlates 1.5--3.6 times more strongly than Dynamic Time Warping, which lacks a gap penalty and therefore cannot distinguish a truncated prediction from a temporally faithful one. EpiCurveBench targets a high-impact public-health application--unlocking decades of outbreak data trapped in published figures--but the benchmark and metric apply directly to any structured time-series chart-extraction setting.

</details>


### [79] [The Coverage Illusion: From Pre-retrieval Routing Failure to Post-retrieval Cascades in a Production RAG System](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27220)
*Zafar Hussain,Kristoffer Nielbo*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文通过丹麦国家百科全书的案例研究，揭示了RAG流水线中查询增强方法存在"覆盖幻觉"现象：合成查询显示90%以上需要LLM增强，但实际生产流量中仅27.8%需要。提出了一种后检索级联策略，以成本最低优先顺序运行工作流，仅在无结果时升级到LLM增强，相比Always-HyDE方案质量提升0.140分，延迟降低31.8%，72.2%的真实用户查询无需LLM增强。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的RAG流水线中对每个查询都应用HyDE和查询扩展等增强方法，导致大量LLM推理成本和端到端延迟增加，但这类开销在实际生产流量中的必要性缺乏实证研究。

Method: 在丹麦国家百科全书中评估了五种检索工作流，使用20,000个查询-工作流对（包含生产流量和合成条件）。提出后检索级联策略：按成本最低优先顺序运行工作流，仅当某一步骤未返回文档时才升级到LLM增强。评估了四种机器学习范式的前检索路由效果。

Result: 合成查询显示90%以上需要LLM增强，但生产流量中仅27.8%需要。前检索路由无法解决这一差距。后检索级联策略在无需训练开销或辅助服务基础设施的情况下，相比Always-HyDE质量提升+0.140 Composite Overall点，延迟降低31.8%，72.2%真实用户查询无需LLM增强。

Conclusion: 存在合成与真实查询分布之间的结构不匹配导致的"覆盖幻觉"，LLM增强需求只有在检索索引后才能揭示，后检索级联是一种高效且实用的解决方案。

Abstract: In modern RAG pipelines, query augmentation methods such as HyDE and query expansion are applied to every query, resulting in substantial LLM inference costs and increased end-to-end latency. The empirical justification for this overhead in real production traffic remains largely unexplored. We present a case study of the Danish National Encyclopedia, evaluating five retrieval workflows over 20,000 query-workflow pairs from production traffic and synthetic conditions. In this system, synthetic queries suggest that LLM augmentation is needed for over 90% of queries to achieve high retrieval coverage. However, under our production deferral policy, only 27.8% of real user queries need LLM augmentation. We call this gap the Coverage Illusion and attribute it to a structural mismatch between synthetic and real query distributions. Pre-retrieval routing cannot resolve this gap, as the need for LLM augmentation is only revealed after searching the index, a result confirmed by our evaluation of four machine learning paradigms. The coverage gap, undetectable from the query alone, motivates a post-retrieval cascade that runs workflows in cheapest-first order and escalates to LLM augmentation only when a step returns no documents. Operating entirely without training overhead or secondary serving infrastructure, the cascade improves quality by +0.140 Composite Overall points over Always-HyDE, reduces latency by 31.8%, and serves 72.2% of real user queries without LLM augmentation.

</details>


### [80] [Temporal Simultaneity Predicts Annotation Quality in Sentiment Corpora](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27239)
*Idris Abdulmumin,Mokgadi Penelope Matloga,Tadesse Destaw Belay,Botshelo Kondowe,Letlhogonolo Mohleleng,Hareaipha Nkopo Letsoalo,Shamsuddeen Hassan Muhammad,Vukosi Marivate*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究Setswana语情感标注数据集中标注者间一致性随时间下降的问题，发现标签混淆集中在消极/中性边界、两名标注者出现自动驾驶式漂移、时间同步性是一致性的主要预测因子，并比较了多种模型在三类情感分类上的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当标注活动持续数周或数月且标注池较小时，标注质量难以维持，亟需理解标注一致性随时间下降的原因。

Method: 使用三名母语标注者对3,565条Setswana语推文分八批次进行情感标注，通过六项针对性分析考察标注者间一致性下降的原因，并基准测试多种开源多语言编码器和商业模型在三类情感分类上的性能。

Result: 批内一致性从总体Kappa=0.76下降超过32个点；标签混淆集中在消极/中性边界；两名标注者出现自动驾驶式漂移；时间同步性是Kappa的主要预测因子（一分钟内标注达0.98，间隔一天以上降至0.65）；微调相比预训练基线提升29-43个macro-F1点，GPT-5少样本学习以62.2 macro-F1领先。

Conclusion: 标注一致性随时间下降主要由时间同步性和标注者漂移驱动，而非语言特征或标注速度；微调可显著提升模型性能；研究为非洲语言NLP资源的可重复质量审计提供了数据集、时间戳和分析代码。

Abstract: Annotation quality is difficult to sustain when campaigns span weeks or months with small annotator pools. We present a Setswana sentiment dataset of 3,565 tweets annotated by three native-speaker annotators across eight batches and examine why inter-annotator agreement (IAA) declines over time. Despite an aggregate Randolph's free-marginal Kappa of $κ= 0.76$, "excellent," per-batch $κ$ falls by more than 32 points across the annotation task. Through six targeted analyses, we find that (i) label confusion concentrates on the negative/neutral boundary, (ii) two annotators show run-length drift consistent with autopilot labeling, and (iii) the dominant predictor of $κ$ is temporal simultaneity: tweets labeled within one minute achieve $κ= 0.98$, while those labeled more than a day apart reach only $κ= 0.65$. Annotation speed and tweet-level linguistic features show no meaningful association with $κ$. We benchmark three open multilingual encoders and proprietary models (GPT-5 and Gemini) on three-class sentiment classification; fine-tuning yields gains of 29 to 43 macro-F1 points over pretrained baselines, with GPT-5 few-shot leading overall (62.2 macro-F1). We release the dataset, per-annotation timestamps, and analysis code to support reproducible quality auditing for future African language NLP resources.

</details>


### [81] [Pair-In, Pair-Out: Latent Multi-Token Prediction for Efficient LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27255)
*Wenhui Tan,Minghao Li,Xiaoqian Ma,Siqi Fan,Xiusheng Huang,Liujie Zhang,Ruihua Song,Weihang Chen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: PIPO通过统一输入侧隐变量压缩和输出侧多令牌预测，并训练轻量级置信头消除验证成本，在多个基准上实现了最高2.64倍的延迟加速和+7.15的pass@4提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法分别从输入侧（隐变量压缩）和输出侧（推测解码、多令牌预测MTP）独立优化推理效率，但输出侧方法需要昂贵的验证器来确认不可靠的草稿令牌。

Method: 提出Pair-In, Pair-Out (PIPO)框架：将隐变量压缩器（折叠两个输入令牌为一个隐表示）与MTP头（展开一个隐状态为一个额外输出令牌）视为镜像操作统一处理；同时训练轻量级置信头决定是否接受草稿令牌，利用On-Policy Distillation (OPD)自然匹配推测解码的拒绝采样准则，以极小额外成本训练置信头。

Result: 在AIME 2025、GPQA-Diamond、LiveCodeBench v6和LongBench v2上，以Qwen3.5-4B和9B为骨干，PIPO的pass@4比常规解码提升最高+7.15点，首令牌延迟加速最高2.64倍，逐令牌延迟加速最高2.07倍。

Conclusion: PIPO通过统一输入输出侧优化并消除验证器开销，有效提升了推理效率与生成质量。

Abstract: Long chain-of-thought reasoning has made autoregressive decoding the dominant inference cost of modern large language models. Existing methods target either the input side (latent compression) or the output side (speculative decoding and multi-token prediction, MTP), but the two lines of work have been pursued independently. Moreover, output-side methods must incur an expensive verifier pass to validate the unreliable draft tokens predicted by MTP. To address these issues, we propose \textbf{Pair-In, Pair-Out (PIPO)}, which unifies both sides by viewing a latent compressor and an MTP head as mirror-image operations: the compressor folds two input tokens into one latent representation, while the MTP head unfolds one hidden state into one additional output token. To remove the verifier cost without sacrificing reliability, PIPO trains a lightweight confidence head that decides whether draft tokens should be accepted. We observe that On-Policy Distillation (OPD) naturally matches the rejection-sampling criterion of speculative decoding, so the confidence head can be trained alongside OPD with negligible extra cost. Experiments on AIME 2025, GPQA-Diamond, LiveCodeBench v6, and LongBench v2 with Qwen3.5-4B and 9B backbones show that PIPO improves pass@4 over regular decoding by up to $+7.15$ points, while delivering up to $2.64\times$ first-token-latency and $2.07\times$ per-token-latency speedups.

</details>


### [82] [Lost in Sampling: Assessing Lexical Reachability in LLMs via the Word Coverage Score (WCS)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27268)
*Samer Awad,Javier Conde,Carlos Arriaga,Tairan Fu,Javier Coronado-Blázquez,Pedro Reviriego*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出词汇覆盖率得分（WCS），量化标准采样过滤器（Top-p、Top-k、Min-p）如何从数学上剔除低频高信息词汇，揭示行业默认采样参数作为非预期审查机制，将人类表达的独特纹理平滑为同质化话语。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM生成文本重复同质，以往研究聚焦模型知识与训练数据，本文探究解码机制对语言多样性的抑制作用。

Method: 提出WCS指标，衡量标准采样过滤器对上下文恰当的人类词汇的数学剪枝程度；通过审计开放权重模型在人类语料片段上的表现，识别解码器在概率空间内使哪些合理词汇不可达。

Result: 行业标准采样默认值充当非预期审查机制，平滑人类表达的独特纹理，导致同质化话语。

Conclusion: WCS提供了优化文本连贯性与词汇丰富度之间权衡的严谨框架，是保留生成模型中人类语言多样性的诊断工具。

Abstract: Modern Large Language Models (LLMs) are often criticized for producing repetitive and homogeneous text, despite possessing vast latent vocabularies. While previous research has focused on model knowledge and training data, we investigate the role of decoding mechanics in suppressing linguistic diversity. We introduce the Word Coverage Score (WCS), a metric that quantifies the extent to which contextually appropriate human vocabulary is mathematically pruned by standard sampling filters (e.g., Top-$p$, Top-$k$, and Min-$p$). Rather than assessing static knowledge, the WCS measures the lexical survival rate of low-frequency, high-information human words as a function of sampling parameters. By auditing open-weight models on human-authored corpus fragments, we identify which logical lexical choices are rendered unreachable by the decoder, even when they reside within the probability space. Our results provide quantitative evidence that industry-standard sampling defaults act as unintended censorship mechanisms, smoothing the unique textures of human expression into a homogenized discourse. The WCS offers a rigorous framework for optimizing the trade-off between text coherence and lexical richness, providing a diagnostic tool for preserving the diversity of human language in generative models.

</details>


### [83] [It's Not Always Sycophancy: Measuring LLM Conformity as a Function of Epistemic Uncertainty](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27288)
*Kevin H. Guo,Chao Yan,Avinash Baidya,Katherine Brown,Xiang Gao,Juming Xiong,Zhijun Yin,Bradley A. Malin*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出MUSE框架，用于解耦LLM在对话中顺从用户压力的两种机制：谄媚性顺从（即使模型对初始回答完全确定，也会因用户压力而改变立场）和不确定性驱动顺从（模型的不确定性越高，越容易顺从用户）。研究发现两种顺从机制随着用户专业感知和用户建议合理性增强而增长。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究将LLM放弃初始立场以顺从用户的行为主要归因于RLHF训练导致的谄媚，但作者假设顺从行为也受到推理时模型 epistemic uncertainty（认知不确定性）的驱动。现有方法未区分这两种机制，因此需要解耦以制定更有针对性的干预策略。

Method: 提出两阶段评估框架MUSE：第一阶段评估模型对查询回答的认知不确定性，第二阶段评估模型在后续对话中顺从用户压力的倾向。通过比较模型在不同不确定性水平下的顺从行为，区分谄媚性顺从（高确定下仍顺从）与不确定性驱动顺从（不确定性增加导致顺从增加）。并通过消融实验分析用户专业感知和用户建议合理性对两种顺从机制的影响。

Result: 1）顺从行为由谄媚性和不确定性两种因素共同驱动，而非仅谄媚；2）两种顺从机制均随LLM感知的用户专业水平和用户建议合理性增强而增长。

Conclusion: MUSE框架能够区分由对齐训练导致的谄媚性顺从和由训练语料分布导致的不确定性驱动顺从，从而为制定更有针对性的干预策略提供指导。理解两种顺从机制的不同来源有助于设计差异化的缓解方案。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are known to abandon their initial stance to conform to user pushback. While prior research largely attributes this behavior to sycophancy learned during reinforcement learning from human feedback, we hypothesize that conformity is also driven by a model's epistemic uncertainty at inference time. In this paper, we introduce MUSE, a two-stage evaluation framework to disentangle the mechanisms driving LLM conformity. Specifically, MUSE maps a model's epistemic uncertainty in responding to a query against its likelihood to yield to user pushback in a subsequent turn. We demonstrate that the mechanisms driving conformity extend beyond sycophancy alone. Specifically, we characterize two distinct factors that jointly drive conformity: sycophantic conformity, where a model aligns with user pushback even with absolute certainty in its initial response, and uncertainty-driven conformity, where a model's likelihood for conformity increases alongside its uncertainty. Furthermore, we conduct ablation studies to demonstrate that both sycophantic conformity and uncertainty-driven conformity grow with 1) the LLM's perceived expertise of the user and 2) the plausibility of the user's suggestions. More broadly, MUSE informs more targeted intervention strategies by distinguishing alignment-induced sycophancy and training-corpora-driven uncertainty.

</details>


### [84] [Separating Semantic Competition from Context Length in RAG Reading](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27294)
*Vyzantinos Repantis,Ameya Gawde,Harshvardhan Singh,Rohit Alekar,Cien Zhang,Svetlana Karslioglu,Akash Vishwakarma*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种用于RAG系统的匹配对照实验协议，通过将难竞争段落替换为较弱的竞争段落（保持数量和长度不变），证明读者模型的失败不仅源于上下文长度，还受到段落间竞争效应的影响。在Phi-2和Qwen2.5-1.5B上的实验显示，该替换能部分恢复性能（如Phi-2的EM提升+6.0，F1提升+0.057），并利用保留曲线和右删失半衰期量化竞争累积效应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究RAG系统中读者模型在检索到正确段落时仍回答错误的原因：究竟是因为上下文变长，还是因为其他段落与正确答案存在竞争关系？现有研究未能将这两种效应分离。

Method: 设计匹配对照协议：固定段落数量和长度不变，将高竞争力干扰段落替换为低竞争力的真实段落。在SQuAD数据集上对Phi-2和Qwen2.5-1.5B两个紧凑开源模型进行实验，采用精确匹配（EM）、F1、答案包含率等指标，并引入保留曲线和右删失半衰期分析竞争累积效应。

Result: 替换竞争段落能部分恢复读者模型性能：Phi-2提升+6.0 EM、+7.0答案包含率、+0.057 F1；Qwen2.5-1.5B提升+4.5 EM、+9.0答案包含率、+0.068 F1。竞争效应对F1和答案包含率的影响比精确匹配更明显，且受片段长度影响。

Conclusion: 匹配对照协议成功分离出独立于上下文长度的竞争效应，证明段落间的竞争是RAG读者模型失败的重要因素，但该效应的强度因评估指标和片段长度而异。

Abstract: Retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) systems can respond incorrectly even when the correct passage was retrieved. The model must still read the retrieved passages and identify which one contains the answer among others that look relevant. This passage-reading model is called the reader. Does it fail simply because the context is longer or because the other passages genuinely compete with the correct one? We introduce and demonstrate a matched-control protocol for RAG reading: we keep the number and length of passages fixed, but replace hard competitors with less competitive real passages. We apply this control across two compact open models on SQuAD. This replacement partially restores performance, with the strongest effects on F1 and answer inclusion. For Phi-2, this recovers +6.0 EM points, +7.0 answer-inclusion points, and +0.057 F1. For Qwen2.5-1.5B, it recovers +4.5 EM points, +9.0 answer-inclusion points, and +0.068 F1. To track how performance changes as competitors accumulate, we also report retention curves and summarize them with a right-censored half-life when the curves do not cross half-retention. Together, these results show the protocol isolates a competition effect distinct from context length, though the effect is clearer for F1 and answer inclusion than for exact match, and also varies with snippet length.

</details>


### [85] [Probing Cultural Awareness in LLMs: A Case Study of Cross-Culture Aesthetic Stylistics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27296)
*Jiashuo Wang,Fenggang Yu,Jian Wang,Chak Tou Leong,Xiaoyu Shen,Chunpu Xu,Jiawen Duan,Wenjie Li,Johan F. Hoorn*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文构建了C4STYLI基准，评估LLM在跨文化语境下对美学风格的识别与生成能力，发现LLM在风格识别上与人类存在差异，且其风格识别主要依赖表层语言信息而非深层风格结构，尤其在香港语境中表现显著。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM被广泛部署于多元文化场景，但其在"美学风格学"（通过语言策略性使用唤起文化共鸣）方面的能力尚未被充分探索。

Method: 构建C4STYLI基准（包含香港与中国大陆高度风格化的翻译电影片名和广告标语），通过行为识别与生成能力评估LLM；采用逻辑回归探针进行结构消融实验，分析LLM风格识别的信息基础。

Result: LLM在风格识别上与人类存在差异，且识别能力因文本领域而异；风格识别与生成性能不一致；在香港语境下，LLM的风格识别主要依赖表层语言信息而非风格结构。

Conclusion: LLM对香港特定风格结构的敏感性有限，其风格识别机制与人类存在本质差异。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) are increasingly deployed in diverse cultural contexts, yet their ability to master aesthetic stylistics, i.e., the strategic use of language to evoke cultural resonance, remains underexplored. We curate C4STYLI, a benchmark of highly stylized translated movie titles and advertising slogans from Hong Kong and the Chinese Mainland, to evaluate LLMs via the lens of behavioral recognition and productive competence. Extensive evaluations show that LLMs differ from humans in stylistic recognition, and this recognition ability varies across text domains. In addition, stylistic recognition and generation performance in LLMs are not consistently aligned. To further examine whether LLMs genuinely capture stylistic information in stylistic recognition, we conduct structural ablation with logistic regression probes. We find that, in the Hong Kong setting, stylistic recognition in LLMs relies primarily on surface-level linguistic information rather than stylistic structure. This suggests limited sensitivity to Hong Kong-specific stylistic structure.

</details>


### [86] [Self-Ensembling Vision-Language Models for Chart Data Extraction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27298)
*Thomas Berkane,Qianyi Wang,Maimuna S. Majumder*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出一种VLM自集成方法，通过对同一图表多次采样表格输出并在单元格级别聚合（取中位数），显著提升图表到表格的提取精度，并引入收敛检测和不确定性估计。同时构建了更复杂的WB-ChartExtract基准，在该基准上实现了最高23%的相对改进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 图表中的数据常以图像形式存在，难以重用和分析；现有VLM方法在处理数据点多、风格多样的图表时性能不佳。

Method: 提出VLM自集成方法：对同一图表多次采样多个表格输出，对齐候选表格后对每个单元格的数值取中位数得到共识表格，并引入收敛检测（停止采样）和不确定性估计（基于样本离散程度）。

Result: 在ChartQA和WB-ChartExtract基准上，该方法优于单次VLM输出，在WB-ChartExtract上最高相对改进达23%。

Conclusion: 该方法有效解锁图表图像中的表格数据，支持下游分析复用，且对复杂图表提升显著。

Abstract: Charts effectively convey quantitative information, but the underlying data are often locked in image form, hindering reuse and analysis. Manually digitizing charts is time-consuming and error-prone, motivating automatic chart-to-table extraction. Recent approaches use specialized vision-language models (VLMs), yet performance still lags on charts with many datapoints or substantial stylistic variation. We propose a VLM self-ensembling method that repeatedly samples multiple tabular outputs from the same VLM for a fixed chart image and aggregates them at the level of individual table cells. We align candidate tables and take per-cell medians over numerical values to produce a more accurate consensus table. Our method also includes convergence detection to stop sampling once the aggregated table stabilizes, and uncertainty estimation based on dispersion across samples to help users assess extraction reliability. Because existing chart extraction benchmarks contain relatively simple plots with limited room for improvement, we introduce WB-ChartExtract, a new benchmark built from World Bank data with more complex and stylistically diverse charts; on average, its charts contain 7 times more datapoints than those in the ChartQA benchmark. Across both ChartQA and WB-ChartExtract, our approach improves extraction accuracy over single-pass VLM outputs, yielding up to 23% relative improvement on WB-ChartExtract after ensembling. More broadly, our method helps unlock tabular data previously siloed in chart images, enabling downstream analysis and reuse.

</details>


### [87] [Chartographer: Counterfactual Chart Generation for Evaluating Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27311)
*Yifan Jiang,Dae Yon Hwang,Jesse C. Cresswell,Freda Shi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出反事实图表框架Chartographer，通过逆向工程将图表转为可执行代码并生成反事实变体，用以严格评估VLM的视觉推理能力，发现模型在单图表上表现良好但无法泛化到反事实变体。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有图表问答基准存在捷径和背景知识干扰，无法严格评估模型的视觉推理能力，需要一种能消除这些干扰的评估方法。

Method: 提出Chartographer框架：将图表逆向工程为可执行代码，验证重构保真度，生成种子控制的反事实变体，并从可执行的QA逻辑中推导新答案。

Result: 反事实图表揭示了单图表性能下隐藏的失败模式：VLM在正确回答原始图表后往往无法泛化到反事实变体，尤其是在需要新的视觉推理路径时失败最严重。

Conclusion: 反事实图表能有效揭示VLM视觉推理的薄弱环节，现有模型在需要真正视觉推理的任务上存在显著不足。

Abstract: Chart question-answering (QA) benchmarks aim to pose questions that require visual reasoning to correctly answer, but models can often reach solutions through shortcuts or prior familiarity with a chart based on their own background knowledge. To strictly evaluate visual reasoning, we propose counterfactual charts where the chart-question task remains fixed, but underlying chart and the corresponding answer are varied. We introduce Chartographer, a framework to reverse engineer charts into executable code, validate reconstruction fidelity, generate seed-controlled counterfactual variants, and derive new answers from executable QA logic. We apply this framework to existing chart QA datasets and evaluate proprietary and open-source vision-language models (VLMs), measuring variation sensitivity and generalizability. Counterfactual charts reveal failures hidden by single-chart performance: VLMs often fail to generalize after answering the original chart correctly. We find failures are most prevalent when updated charts require novel visual reasoning pathways.

</details>


### [88] [When Does Demographic Information Help? Data and Modeling Regimes for Perspective-Aware Hate Speech Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27313)
*Weibin Cai,Reza Zafarani*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文系统分析了人口统计学特征在主观标注任务（如仇恨言论检测）中有助于模型性能的条件，发现其收益取决于数据划分特性和建模框架，并提出了一个门控残差模型来选择性利用人口统计学信息。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人口统计学信息在主观任务（如仇恨言论检测）中用于建模标注者视角，但其收益不一致：有时提升性能，有时表现为噪声。本文旨在系统分析人口统计学特征何时有助于模型性能。

Method: 1. 分析人口统计学收益与数据划分特性（标注者分歧度、训练集大小、训练-测试集人口统计学覆盖度）及建模框架的关系。2. 提出门控人口残差模型（gated demographic residual model），将人口统计学信息作为对纯文本预测的选择性调整。

Result: 人口统计学收益集中在以下场景：低训练分歧、高测试分歧、细粒度歧义测量、充足训练数据、高人口统计学重叠。门控人口残差模型在MHS和POPQUORN数据集上有效，尤其在高分歧或低置信度样本上表现突出。

Conclusion: 人口统计学特征不应默认有用，其价值取决于数据划分特性和建模框架的联合作用。

Abstract: Demographic information is often used to model annotator perspectives in subjective tasks such as hate speech detection, but its benefit is inconsistent: it improves performance in some settings and behaves as noise in others. This paper asks when demographic features help. We analyze demographic gain as a function of both data split properties and modeling frameworks. For data splits, we measure annotator disagreement, namely how often annotators assign different labels to the same example, along with training size and train-test demographic coverage. We find that demographic gains concentrate in regimes with low training disagreement, high test disagreement, fine-grained ambiguity measurement, sufficient training data, and greater demographic overlap. Motivated by these regimes, we introduce a gated demographic residual model that treats demographics as a selective adjustment to text-only predictions. Experiments on MHS and POPQUORN show that this design is effective, especially on high disagreement or low confidence examples. Overall, our results suggest that demographics should not be assumed useful by default; their value depends jointly on the data regime and the modeling framework.

</details>


### [89] [Real Images, Worse Judgments: Evaluating Vision-Language Models on Concreteness and Imagery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27315)
*Yifan Jiang,Ruoxi Ning,Sheng Yao,Freda Shi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究了视觉语言模型（VLMs）在词汇判断任务中，真实图像上下文不仅未能提升反而常损害与人类评分的对齐，尤其在视觉证据最不相关的抽象/低意象词上表现明显，表明当前VLMs需要更好地校准视觉上下文的使用时机。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 质疑"视觉输入通常能改善多模态模型的语言理解"这一常见假设，探究VLMs能否区分有用的视觉证据与无关的图像背景信息。

Method: 使用人类具体性和意象评分作为基准，比较VLMs在有/无真实图像上下文时的表现；通过探针分析、典型相关分析（CCA）及归因案例研究，检测表征变化和对伪视觉线索的敏感性。

Result: 真实图像上下文未带来一致增益，在视觉证据最不相关的词上损害最大；图像上下文导致表征偏移和对伪视觉线索更敏感，削弱了目标词汇属性的可恢复性；推理时指令模型仅关注文本内容可减轻这种退化。

Conclusion: 当前指令微调的VLMs需要更好的校准机制，以判断何时视觉上下文应参与词汇判断任务。

Abstract: Visual inputs are often assumed to improve language understanding in multimodal models. We examine this assumption by asking whether vision-language models (VLMs) can distinguish useful visual evidence from incidental image context in lexical judgments. We use human concreteness and imagery ratings because they span words with varying expected visual relevance, from abstract and low-imagery words to concrete and high-imagery words. We find that real-image contexts do not yield consistent gains and often hurt alignment with human ratings, most sharply when visual evidence is least relevant. Through probing and canonical correlation analysis, complemented by an attribution case study, we find that real-image contexts are associated with representational shifts and greater sensitivity to spurious visual cues, coinciding with weaker recoverability of the targeted lexical properties. We further show that instructing models to focus solely on textual content at inference time can reduce this degradation, with the clearest gains on these vulnerable subsets. Our findings suggest that current instruction-tuned VLMs need better calibration of when visual context should inform lexical judgments.

</details>


### [90] [Semantic Gradients Interactions in SSD: A Case Study in Racial Identity and Hate Speech](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27322)
*Felix Ostrowicki,Hubert Plisiecki*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出交互式语义分化（interaction SSD）方法，扩展了监督语义分化（SSD），用于建模语义意义在不同调节变量（如群体、特征或条件）间的变异，并使其可检验、可解释。该方法估计主语义梯度、交互梯度和条件梯度，均可通过标准SSD工具解释。在UC Berkeley仇恨言论语料库上的实验表明，标注者的种族身份显著调节了对针对有色人种评论的仇恨言论判断。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有监督语义分化（SSD）方法无法建模语义意义在不同群体或条件下的系统性变异，缺乏对调节效应的统计检验和解释能力。

Method: 在标准SSD框架中引入交互项，估计主语义梯度（共享语义空间）、交互梯度（调节变量引起的变异）和条件梯度（各条件下的语义梯度），所有梯度均保持标准SSD的可解释性。

Result: 在UC Berkeley仇恨言论语料库中检测到显著的调节效应：主梯度区分"非人化敌意"与"反言论"，交互梯度揭示了不同种族群体在哪些语义线索预测仇恨言论评分上存在较小的组间差异。

Conclusion: 交互式SSD使得调节性的意义-结果关系变得统计可检验且可解释，为研究语义意义在不同条件下的系统性变化提供了方法论工具。

Abstract: We introduce interaction SSD, an extension of Supervised Semantic Differential that models how semantic meaning varies across moderators such as groups, traits, or conditions making this variation testable and interpretable. The method estimates a main semantic gradient, an interaction gradient, and conditional gradients, all interpretable through standard SSD tools. We illustrate it on the UC Berkeley Measuring Hate Speech corpus, testing whether annotator racial identity moderates hate-speech judgments of comments targeting people of color. The interaction model detects a significant moderation effect: the shared gradient contrasts dehumanizing hostility with counter-speech, while the interaction gradient reveals smaller group-linked differences in which semantic cues predict hate-speech ratings. Interaction SSD makes moderated meaning-outcome relationships statistically testable and interpretable.

</details>


### [91] [FinHarness: An Inline Lifecycle Safety Harness for Finance LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27333)
*Haoxuan Jia,Yang Liu,Bin Chong,Yingguang Yang,Yancheng Chen,Jiayu Liang,Qian Li,Hanning Lu,Kefu Xu,Hao Zheng,Chongyang Zhang,Hao Peng,Philip S. Yu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: FinHarness是一个面向金融LLM Agent的内联安全框架，通过查询监控、工具监控和级联模块实现多步业务流程中恶意行为的实时阻断与良性操作的保留，在FinVault数据集上将攻击成功率从38.3%降至15.0%，同时仅轻微降低良性批准率（41.1%→39.3%），并比始终使用高级法官的方案节省4.7倍的计算成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有边界过滤器无法拦截中间轨迹中的不可逆工具调用，而事后LLM审计仅在终止后执行，无法进行干预且计算成本随轨迹长度线性增长，亟需一种能够实时拦截恶意操作并保留合法多步工作流的金融Agent安全机制。

Method: 提出FinHarness内联安全框架，包含三个组件：①查询监控（Query Monitor）：融合单轮意图与跨轮漂移检测；②工具监控（Tool Monitor）：评估每个即将执行的工具调用；③级联模块（Cascade）：整合每步风险，在轻量级与高级LLM法官之间自适应路由验证。触发的风险因子作为事前证据重新注入Agent输入，使Agent自主选择拒绝、重规划或批准。

Result: 在FinVault数据集上，路由后的FinHarness将攻击成功率从38.3%降至15.0%，良性批准率从41.1%轻微下降至39.3%，且高级法官调用次数比始终使用高级法官的消融实验减少4.7倍。

Conclusion: FinHarness通过内联实时监控与级联路由机制，在保障金融Agent多步业务流程合法性的同时有效阻断恶意操作，并显著降低高级LLM计算成本。

Abstract: Finance LLM agents must simultaneously block prompt-induced unauthorized actions and approve legitimate multi-step business workflows. However, boundary filters often miss irreversible mid-trajectory tool calls, while post-hoc LLM judges perform auditing only after termination -- too late for intervention and at a computational cost that scales linearly with trace length. We present FinHarness, an inline safety harness that wraps a finance agent end-to-end with three components: a Query Monitor that fuses single-turn intent with cross-turn drift, a Tool Monitor that evaluates each prospective tool call, and a Cascade module that integrates per-step risk and adaptively routes verification between a lightweight and an advanced-tier LLM judge. Fired risk factors are re-injected into the agent input as ex-ante evidence, enabling the agent to refuse, re-plan, or approve on its own. On FinVault, routed FinHarness cuts ASR from 38.3% to 15.0% while largely preserving benign approval ($41.1\% \to 39.3\%$), and uses $4.7\times$ fewer advanced-judge calls than an always-advanced ablation.

</details>


### [92] [MATCHA: Matching Text via Contrastive Semantic Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27345)
*Siran Li,Ece Sena Etoglu,Carsten Eickhoff,Seyed Ali Bahrainian*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文指出现有评估指标（ROUGE、BERTScore等）无法区分语义矛盾，提出MATCHA——一种联合奖励语义一致性与惩罚矛盾的双视角自动评估指标，在多个基准上优于现有方法，在TruthfulQA上相比ROUGE-L提升18.38%、相比BERTScore提升20.82%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM评估指标（如ROUGE、BERTScore）常将相互矛盾的文本评为相似分数，掩盖了模型输出的根本性错误，亟需能同时评估语义一致性和检测矛盾的可靠评估方法。

Method: MATCHA采用双视角评估：一方面衡量与黄金参考文本的语义接近度（proximity），另一方面衡量与对抗生成的矛盾文本的语义距离（distance），通过联合优化实现对语义一致性的奖励和对矛盾内容的惩罚。

Result: 在8个公开基准（涵盖问答、图像描述、自然语言推理、摘要、语义文本相似性）上，MATCHA优于流行指标。在TruthfulQA上，MATCHA相比ROUGE-L提升18.38%，相比BERTScore提升20.82%。与23个嵌入模型对比，MATCHA在区分正确与错误陈述方面最为准确。

Conclusion: MATCHA通过同时考虑与参考文本的接近度和与矛盾文本的距离，比传统指标更准确地评估LLM输出质量，揭示了现有评估指标的固有缺陷。代码已公开。

Abstract: Reliable evaluation is essential for understanding large language model (LLM) performance, yet today's go-to metrics, namely token-overlap scores (e.g., ROUGE) and embedding-based measures (e.g., BERTScore), often misjudge semantic similarity of documents. Our study shows that both token-overlap metrics and embedding-based metrics routinely assign nearly identical scores to texts that directly contradict each other, thereby potentially masking fundamental errors. We introduce MATCHA, an automatic metric that jointly rewards semantic agreement with a reference and penalizes contradictions. MATCHA employs a dual-view perspective that measures (i) proximity to the gold text and (ii) distance from an adversarially generated counterfactual contradiction. In eight public benchmarks, MATCHA outperforms popular metrics, compared with human annotations on question-answering, image caption generation, natural language inference, summarization, and semantic textual similarity tasks. On the TruthfulQA dataset (i.e., a dataset without a training set, where no embedding-based metrics could locally train on), this improvement in terms of matching texts with a reference reaches 18.38% over ROUGE-L and 20.82% over BERTScore. Both quantitative comparison and qualitative human assessments confirm the efficacy and validity of MATCHA and uncover fundamental weaknesses in pre-existing metrics. Compared with 23 embedding models, including top state-of-the-art ones, used as a metric similar to BERTScore, MATCHA remains the most accurate in distinguishing correct from incorrect statements solely based on a reference. Our code and metric are publicly available (https://github.com/Siran-Li/MATCHA).

</details>


<div id='cs.CR'></div>

# cs.CR [[Back]](#toc)

### [93] [MemMorph: Tool Hijacking in LLM Agents via Memory Poisoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26154)
*Xuanye Zhang,Yongsen Zheng,Zhuqin Xu,Kaiyu Zhou,Bowen Shen,Haoran Ou,Tianwei Zhang,Kwok-Yan Lam*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出MemMorph，首个通过毒化智能体长期记忆来偏斜工具选择的攻击方法，仅需注入3条伪装记录即可达到高达85.9%的攻击成功率，揭示了长期记忆作为工具增强型智能体中一个关键且尚未被充分探索的攻击面。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有攻击主要操纵工具元数据，容易被审计检测，且随着智能体越来越多地采用记忆模块通过积累经验来优化工具选择策略，这些攻击可能失效。因此需要探索针对长期记忆的攻击方法。

Method: MemMorph通过向智能体的长期记忆中注入少量精心构造的记录（伪装成技术事实、事故报告和操作策略），这些毒化记录重塑智能体的上下文感知和决策过程，使其自主推断并选择攻击者偏好的工具，而非显式指令化工具调用决策。

Result: 在3个基准、10个智能体骨干和3种记忆模块实现上，MemMorph仅注入3条记录即可达到高达85.9%的攻击成功率，比最强基线高出25%，且在3种代表性防御下仍保持有效性。

Conclusion: 长期记忆是工具增强型智能体中一个关键且尚未被充分探索的攻击面，亟需开发记忆层级的完整性保护机制。

Abstract: LLM-driven agents are capable of selecting external tools to complete users' tasks. However, attackers could compromise such process, steering agents toward inappropriate/wrong tools and enabling malicious actions. Most existing attacks primarily manipulate the tool metadata, which is easily detectable by auditing and may lose effectiveness as modern agents increasingly adopt memory modules to refine tool selection policies through accumulated experience. This paper proposes MemMorph, the first attack that bias tool selection by poisoning the agent's long-term memory. Rather than explicitly dictating the tool invocation decision, MemMorph injects a small number of crafted records that are disguised as technical facts, incident reports, and operational policies. These poisoned records reshape the agent's contextual perception and decision-making process, leading it to autonomously infer and select the tool preferred by the attacker. Experiments across 3 benchmarks, 10 agent backbones, and 3 memory-module implementations show that MemMorph achieves up to 85.9% attack success rate with only three injected records, outperforming the strongest baseline by up to 25% while retaining potency under 3 representative defenses. Our findings expose long-term memory as a critical and under-explored attack surface in tool-augmented agents, urging the development of memory-level integrity safeguards.

</details>


### [94] [Turning Bias into Bugs: Bandit-Guided Style Manipulation Attacks on LLM Judges](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26156)
*Xianglin Yang,Bryan Hooi,Gelei Deng,Tianwei Zhang,Jin Song Dong*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出BITE，一种黑盒对抗框架，通过语义保持的文本风格编辑来欺骗LLM裁判，人为提高其评分。该方法将风格编辑选择建模为上下文多臂赌博机问题，使用LinUCB策略自适应选择能最大化评分的编辑。实验表明，BITE在多种LLM裁判和任务上实现了超过65%的攻击成功率，可将评分提高1-2分（9分制），同时能规避风格控制和检测基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM裁判存在已知的风格偏差（如偏好冗长或特定句子结构），但这种偏差尚未被充分探索为安全漏洞。本文旨在揭示并利用这一弱点，证明LLM-as-a-judge范式的脆弱性。

Method: 提出BITE（BIas exploraTion and Exploitation）黑盒对抗框架：将风格编辑选择建模为上下文多臂赌博机问题，使用LinUCB策略在无需模型参数或梯度的情况下自适应选择能最大化裁判评分的语义保持编辑。

Result: 在多种LLM裁判和任务（包括点式和成对比较）上，BITE攻击成功率超过65%，评分提升1-2分（9分制），并能规避标准风格控制方法和多种检测基线。

Conclusion: BITE暴露了LLM-as-a-judge范式的根本性弱点，强调了开发能够抵抗此类攻击的评估方法的必要性。

Abstract: The known stylistic biases in LLM judges, such as a preference for verbosity or specific sentence structures, present an underexplored security vulnerability. In this work, we introduce BITE (BIas exploraTion and Exploitation), a black-box adversarial framework that learns semantics-preserving edits to mislead an LLM judge and artificially inflate the scores it assigns. We cast the selection of stylistic edits as a contextual bandit problem and use a LinUCB policy to adaptively choose edits that maximize the judge's score without access to model parameters or gradients. Empirically, we test BITE across a diverse range of LLM judges and tasks, including both pointwise and pairwise comparisons on chatbot leaderboards and AI-reviewer benchmarks. BITE achieves an attack success rate exceeding 65% and raises scores by 1-2 points on a 9-point scale, all while preserving semantic equivalence. We further assess the attack's stealthiness, showing that BITE evades standard style-control methods and several detection baselines. Our findings expose a fundamental weakness in the LLM-as-a-judge paradigm and motivate robust, attack-aware evaluation. Our code is available at https://github.com/xianglinyang/llm-as-a-judge-attack.

</details>


### [95] [Furina: Fragmented Uncertainty-Driven Refusal Instability Attack](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26158)
*Tongxi Wu,Jian Zhang,Yang Gao*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文挑战了LLM/MLLM安全对齐作为近二值阈值机制的假设，发现安全行为存在不稳定性区域，小扰动即可导致随机的拒绝决策。作者提出了多指标诊断框架，并基于此设计了Furina越狱攻击，通过碎片化、场景锚定的提示诱导安全不稳定特征，在多个基准上取得优异效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究普遍认为安全对齐是一个近乎二值的阈值机制，但这忽略了安全行为在一定扰动下可能呈现随机性的不稳定区域，导致检测性防御在面对复杂攻击时失效。

Method: 构建了结合外部信号（输出不确定性）与内部信号（安全激活状态）的多指标诊断框架，识别出不稳定区域的典型特征：输出不确定性升高但内部安全激活降低的解耦现象。基于此设计了Furina攻击，通过碎片化场景锚定提示诱导该特征，无需模型特定优化。

Result: Furina在HarmBench上优于强单轮和多轮基线方法，在MM-SafetyBench上取得有竞争力的结果，验证了不确定性放大作为理解安全漏洞的可迁移机制的有效性。

Conclusion: 安全行为受制于不稳定性区域而非确定性二进制阈值，检测性防御因无法应对输出不确定性与内部安全激活的解耦而失效；不确定性放大是一种可迁移的安全漏洞攻击机制。

Abstract: Safety alignment in large language models (LLMs) and multimodal large language models (MLLMs) is commonly assumed to operate as a near-binary threshold mechanism. We challenge this assumption by revealing that safety behavior is governed by an instability region where small perturbations induce stochastic refusal decisions rather than deterministic outcomes. We develop a multi-metric diagnostic framework combining external and internal signals to characterize this instability. Through systematic experiments, we identify a characteristic diagnostic signature: inputs in unstable regimes exhibit elevated output uncertainty yet decreased internal safety activation, a decoupling phenomenon that explains why detection-based defenses fail against sophisticated attacks. Building on this framework, we introduce Furina, a jailbreak attack that deliberately induces this signature through fragmented, scene-anchored prompts without model-specific optimization. Furina outperforms strong single-turn and multi-turn baselines on HarmBench and achieves competitive results on MM-SafetyBench, demonstrating that uncertainty amplification provides a principled and transferable mechanism for understanding safety vulnerabilities. Code is available at: https://github.com/0xCavaliers/Furina_Jailbreak.

</details>


### [96] [Enhancing Autonomous Online Intrusion Detection for IoT with Balanced Learning, Reliable Pseudo-Labels, and Lightweight Architectures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26166)
*Hanzala Afzaal,Danish Memon,Chouhdary Bilal Raza,Muhammad Khurram Shahzad*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文复现了INFOCOM 2024的AOC-IDS在线入侵检测系统，识别其四大缺陷（类别不平衡、伪标签不可靠、泛化性差、计算开销大），并提出XGBoost-BalSamp方法（准确率95.45%）和深度学习组合改进（PseudoFilter+MixupAug+LiteAE，准确率90.88%，参数量减少55%），显著优于基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 物联网设备激增催生了对自适应、资源高效的在线入侵检测系统的迫切需求，现有AOC-IDS虽为前沿方案但存在性能瓶颈和部署限制，亟需针对性改进以提升准确率和边缘设备适用性。

Method: 1) 复现AOC-IDS（AE+CRC损失+高斯决策模块）；2) 诊断四类缺陷；3) 提出XGBoost-BalSamp（处理类别不平衡）；4) 提出深度学习组合PseudoFilter（伪标签过滤）+MixupAug（数据增强）+LiteAE（轻量自编码器，参数量减少55%）。

Result: XGBoost-BalSamp在UNSW-NB15上达95.45%准确率，提升6.26%；深度学习组合最佳达90.88%准确率（F1:91.45%），参数量减少55%。

Conclusion: 针对AOC-IDS的定向改进可稳定提升检测精度，同时显著增强在物联网边缘设备上的实用部署能力。

Abstract: The rapid proliferation of Internet of Things (IoT) devices has created an urgent demand for adaptive, resource-efficient Intrusion Detection Systems (IDS) capable of handling dynamic and evolving cyber threats. This paper investigates AOC-IDS, a state-of-the-art autonomous online IDS published at IEEE INFOCOM 2024, which employs an Autoencoder (AE) with Cluster Repelling Contrastive (CRC) loss and an autonomous Gaussian-based decision module. We first successfully replicate AOC-IDS on the UNSW-NB15 benchmark, achieving 89.39% accuracy in close agreement with the published 89.19%. We then identify four key limitations: class imbalance, unreliable pseudo-label generation, limited generalization, and computational overhead for IoT deployment, and propose targeted improvements for each. Our XGBoost-BalSamp method achieves 95.45% accuracy on UNSW-NB15, a gain of 6.26% over the baseline. Our combined deep learning approach (PseudoFilter, MixupAug, and LiteAE) achieves a best-run accuracy of 90.88% (F1: 91.45%), surpassing the base paper while reducing model parameters by 55%.These results demonstrate that targeted improvements to AOC-IDS yield consistent accuracy gains while improving practical deployability on IoT edge devices.

</details>


### [97] [AgentSecBench: Measuring Prompt Injection, Privacy Leakage, and Tool-Use Integrity in LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26269)
*Faruk Alpay,Taylan Alpay*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出AgentSecBench框架，将LLM Agent中指令/检索/工具观测通过同一生成通道处理的安全问题形式化为三个安全游戏（指令完整性、检索机密性、能力完整性），基于“意图到执行无干扰”原则，区分策略描述与策略实施，并通过实验验证六类防御方法的效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM Agent将可信指令、检索记录和工具观测通过同一生成通道处理，导致数据流与权限混淆：不受信任的字符串可能影响机密响应或动作提案，即使没有应用策略授权这种影响。现有方法缺乏形式化的安全框架来建模和评估这一问题。

Method: 定义三个安全游戏（指令完整性、检索机密性、能力完整性），基于意图到执行无干扰与允许泄露的统一概念；将应用策略表示为对授权观测和能力的投影，区分提示注释与强制投影；通过精确标记实验测试披露和禁止动作区分器；使用Qwen3-0.6B和Qwen3-1.7B评估六类防御方法的对抗优势。

Result: 实验测量展示了风险降低何时跟随通道关闭，以及模型可见的对抗能力何时仍然可利用。

Conclusion: 提出了一种面向安全性的评估方法：提示文本可以描述安全边界，而来源投影、能力限制和输出验证可以强制实施安全边界。

Abstract: LLM agents process trusted instructions, retrieved records, and tool observations through a common generative channel. This conflates data flow with authority: an untrusted string can affect a secret-bearing response or an action proposal even when no application policy authorizes that influence. We introduce AgentSecBench as an empirical instantiation of a formal security framework for this problem. The framework defines three games-instruction-integrity, retrieval-confidentiality, and capability-integrity-under a common notion of intent-to-execution noninterference with permitted leakage. It represents an application policy as a projection onto authorized observations and capabilities, distinguishes prompt annotations from enforcing projections, and measures both adversarial advantage and whether a defense closes the relevant model-visible channel before generation. The exact-marker experiments are intentionally one observable instantiation of the games rather than a complete semantic security claim: they test disclosure and forbidden-action distinguishers with unambiguous ground truth. We evaluate six defense classes with Qwen3-0.6B and Qwen3-1.7B on paired adversarial and benign-control executions. The measurements show when risk reduction follows channel closure and when a model-visible adversarial capability remains exploitable. The result is a security-oriented evaluation method: prompt text can describe a boundary, whereas provenance projection, capability restriction, and output validation can enforce one.

</details>


### [98] [Context-Aware Metric Differential Privacy for Vehicle Trajectory Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26351)
*Gaoyi Chen,Yan Huang,Chenxi Qiu*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出上下文感知的度量差分隐私（C-mDP）框架，通过将上下文依赖融入隐私定义和效用模型，并利用条件独立性结构简化线性规划求解，在车辆位置隐私保护中实现了比标准mDP更高的效用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有度量差分隐私（mDP）机制独立扰动每个位置记录，未建模上下文信息（如历史移动轨迹）对发布数据效用的影响，这在时间相关的车辆轨迹场景中尤为突出。

Method: 提出C-mDP框架，将受保护秘密视为上下文增强记录，在该增强域上施加度量不可区分性；将最优机制设计建模为线性规划；利用当前位置与上下文变量间的条件独立性结构进行降维。

Result: 在真实车辆移动数据集上，C-mDP在相同隐私预算下始终比标准mDP基线实现更高的效用，同时满足所需的度量隐私保证。

Conclusion: 通过显式建模上下文依赖关系，C-mDP能够在保证严格隐私的前提下显著提升位置数据的效用，为时间相关场景下的位置隐私保护提供了有效方案。

Abstract: Metric Differential Privacy (mDP) generalizes differential privacy by allowing privacy guarantees to be expressed with respect to an arbitrary distance metric over secrets. While mDP has been adopted in geo-location protection, most existing mechanisms perturb each location record in isolation and do not model how contextual information (e.g., recent mobility history) affects the utility of the released data. This mismatch is particularly pronounced for vehicle mobility traces, where service quality often depends on temporally correlated locations.
  In this paper, we propose Context-aware mDP (C-mDP), a framework for vehicle location privacy that incorporates contextual dependencies into both the utility model and the privacy notion. C-mDP treats the protected secret as a context-augmented record and enforces metric indistinguishability over this augmented domain. We formulate optimal C-mDP mechanism design as a linear program (LP) that minimizes expected utility loss subject to C-mDP constraints. To improve scalability, we exploit conditional-independence structure between the current location and contextual variables to derive a reduced formulation with substantially fewer decision variables and constraints. We evaluate C-mDP on real-world vehicle mobility datasets and compare it with standard mDP baselines. The results show that C-mDP consistently achieves higher utility under the same privacy budget while satisfying the required metric privacy guarantees.

</details>


### [99] [Jailbreak susceptibility prediction and mitigation via the behavioral geometry of models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26409)
*Hayden Helm,Xiaodong Liu,Weiwei Yang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种行为几何框架，通过利用已评估和防御过的模型，实现对模型群体中越狱攻击的易感性高效预测和防御迁移。在79个模型（24个提供商）和100个系统配置上的实验表明，该方法能以约98%更少的探测达到0.94 AUPRC的易感性检测，并显著优于同提供商分配的防御迁移效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 由于可部署系统数量庞大，对每个配置进行全面评估和优化是不切实际的。因此，需要一种能够利用已有评估和防御结果，在模型群体中高效预测越狱攻击易感性和迁移防御策略的方法。

Method: 形式化定义了一个模型群体的行为几何（behavioral geometry），基于已评估和防御过的模型，支持对未评估模型的易感性预测以及优化防御策略在群体中的迁移。实验覆盖了24个提供商的79个模型和一个基础模型的100种系统配置。

Result: 简单方法基于行为几何即可达到0.94 AUPRC的易感性检测，相比全面评估减少了约98%的探测次数。在防御迁移方面，基于行为几何选择迁移源模型优于同提供商分配（+2%，p=0.03），且3个模型即可覆盖整个群体。结果对超参数选择和评估法官（judge）具有鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 行为几何框架能够有效支持模型群体层面的越狱攻击易感性预测和防御迁移，显著降低评估成本，且具有良好的鲁棒性和可扩展性。

Abstract: Evaluating and mitigating a generative system's susceptibility to jailbreak attacks is critical to its safe deployment. Given the number of deployable systems, full per-configuration evaluation and optimization is impractical. In this paper, we formalize the behavioral geometry of a population of models that, by leveraging previously evaluated and defended models, supports both efficient susceptibility prediction and effective defense transfer across a population. We apply the framework to 79 models spanning 24 providers and to 100 system configurations of a single base model. Simple methods that use the behavioral geometry reach an AUPRC of $0.94$ for susceptibility detection with $\approx98\%$ fewer probes relative to a full evaluation. Using the behavioral geometry to select which model to transfer an optimized defense from outperforms same-provider assignment ($+2\%$, $p = 0.03$) at no additional probe cost, with a set of three models sufficient to cover the population. Results are robust to hyperparameter selection and judge.

</details>


### [100] [Beyond Epsilon: A Principled QIF Framework for Local Differential Privacy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26465)
*Ramon G. Gonze,Natasha Fernandes,Heber H. Arcolezi,Catuscia Palamidessi,Nataliia Bielova*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出基于量化信息流（QIF）的框架，通过Blackwell序（精炼序）对七种主流本地差分隐私（LDP）频率估计协议进行系统比较，揭示此前被认为"最优"的协议实际可能被其他协议严格支配或不可比较，从而在LDP与形式化方法之间建立桥梁。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前LDP协议比较缺乏系统性和原则性方式：ε参数仅约束最坏情况区分性，而效用驱动分析依赖于特定数据集和任务。两者均未能考虑协议面对不同攻击者模型的强度，需要一种更本质、与攻击者无关的比较框架。

Method: 将LDP机制建模为概率信道，利用量化信息流（QIF）理论和Blackwell序（精炼序）建立协议间的偏序关系。当协议P的精炼序优于协议Q时，P在所有可能攻击者下均不差于Q。对GRR、BLH、OLH、SUE、OUE、THE等七种协议进行形式化分析。

Result: 发现某些此前被视为"最优"的协议实际上与其他协议不可比较或被严格支配；给出了七种协议之间的精炼序关系图，揭示了协议间更为精细的强弱关系。

Conclusion: QIF框架为LDP协议提供了与攻击者无关的、基于信息论的形式化比较方法，弥合了LDP与形式化方法社区之间的鸿沟，支持对本地隐私系统进行原则性的、对抗感知的推理。

Abstract: Local Differential Privacy (LDP) has become the de facto standard for privacy-preserving data collection in large-scale systems, in particular for the purpose of estimating frequencies. However, the current research landscape lacks a systematic and principled way to compare LDP protocols. The parameter $\varepsilon$ of LDP is considered the measure of privacy, but it only bounds worst-case distinguishability. Other comparisons rely on utility-driven analyses, where mechanisms are ranked based on their ability to preserve data utility for a given privacy budget $\varepsilon$. Both such kinds of comparisons fail to account for the strength of protocols against diverse attacker models. In this paper, we propose a framework for analyzing LDP frequency estimation protocols through the lens of Quantitative Information Flow (QIF). By modeling LDP mechanisms as probabilistic channels, we leverage the concept of refinement (Blackwell ordering) to establish more principled classifications. This approach allows us to determine when one protocol is intrinsically superior to another for all possible adversaries, and to discuss the implications for utility. In particular, our analysis uncovers cases where protocols previously deemed "optimal" are, in fact, incomparable with, or strictly dominated by, other protocols. We provide a formal QIF-based treatment of seven state-of-the-art protocols, including Generalized Randomized Response (GRR), local hashing variants (BLH, OLH), unary encoding schemes (SUE, OUE), and Thresholding with Histogram Encoding (THE). This perspective bridges the gap between the LDP and formal methods communities and enables principled, adversary-aware reasoning about locally private systems.

</details>


### [101] [GradSentry: Gradient Spectral Entropy for Backdoor Sample Filtering in Large Language Model Fine-Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26574)
*Haodong Zhao,Tianyi Xu,Tianhang Zhao,Zhuosheng Zhang,Gongshen Liu*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: GradSentry提出基于每个样本梯度谱熵的防御方法，用于检测大语言模型微调中的后门样本。该方法无需聚类，在1%-90%的投毒比例下均有效，计算开销低（7B模型每样本20-50ms），在多个数据集和攻击类型上验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型在不可信数据上微调容易遭受后门攻击，现有的基于聚类的样本过滤防御方法需要足够的数据量且在极端投毒比例下会失效，亟需一种更鲁棒且高效的防御方法。

Method: GradSentry利用每个样本梯度的谱熵来区分干净样本和后门样本。核心发现是中毒样本产生的梯度具有比干净样本更高的谱熵。该方法通过计算单个样本梯度的谱特征捕获后门签名，避免了成对样本比较和聚类过程。该方法与训练方式无关，兼容LoRA和全参数微调。

Result: 在四个QA数据集和四种攻击类型上的实验验证了谱熵对后门检测的有效性。方法在1%-90%的投毒比例下均能有效工作，计算开销仅为每样本20-50ms（7B模型），优于依赖聚类的现有防御方法。

Conclusion: GradSentry通过梯度谱熵提供了一种高效、鲁棒且训练无关的后门样本过滤方案，在极端投毒比例下仍保持有效性，且计算开销低，具有良好的实用价值。

Abstract: Fine-tuning Large Language Models with untrusted data exposes models to backdoor attacks, where poisoned samples cause targeted misbehavior. Existing sample-filtering defenses rely on clustering, which requires sufficient data and can fail at extreme poison ratios. We propose GradSentry ({Grad}ient {Sentry}), a backdoor sample filtering method based on the spectral entropy of per-sample gradients. Our key finding is that poisoned samples produce gradients with higher spectral entropy compared to clean samples. GradSentry captures output-altering backdoor signatures using per-sample gradient spectra, avoiding pairwise sample comparisons and clustering during feature construction. Importantly, our method is training-agnostic: it works for both parameter-efficient fine-tuning methods like LoRA and full-parameter tuning, as the gradient analysis operates independently of which parameters are being updated during training. GradSentry requires no clustering, operates effectively across all poison ratios (1%--90%), and introduces minimal computational overhead (20-50ms per sample for 7B model). Evaluation on four QA datasets and four attack types demonstrates the effectiveness of spectral entropy for backdoor detection. Code is available at https://github.com/dongdongzhaoUP/GradSentry.

</details>


### [102] [Cordyceps: Covert Control Attacks on LLMs via Data Poisoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26595)
*Zedian Shao,Charles Fleming,Teodora Baluta*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出一种新型数据投毒攻击方法——隐蔽控制攻击，通过语义关联在LLM中植入信息隐藏机制，使其能够编码和解码任意恶意指令，且能规避多种主流防御手段。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有投毒攻击依赖固定触发短语，容易被异常检测、干净数据正则化或在线监控等防御手段识别和中和，亟需更隐蔽且鲁棒的攻击方法。

Method: 利用共享知识（事实或概念）与攻击者选定短语之间的语义关联，在LLM微调过程中植入信息隐藏方案，使模型能够隐蔽地编码和解码任意恶意指令。

Result: 在5个LLM、3种后门防御和4种提示注入防御上评估，以小比例投毒数据即可比启发式提示注入攻击平均成功率高出约40%，经后门防御后仍保持高达93%成功率，经提示注入防御后高达98%。

Conclusion: 隐蔽控制攻击揭示了LLM微调过程中一种新的、微妙的投毒诱导漏洞，现有防御手段难以有效抵御。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are often fine-tuned on uncurated text datasets that adversaries can poison. Existing poisoning attacks primarily rely on fixed trigger phrases that defenses such as outlier detection, clean-data regularization, or online monitoring can neutralize. In this paper, we propose a data poisoning method that teaches an LLM an information hiding scheme reliably and stealthily through semantic associations between shared knowledge such as facts or concepts and attacker-chosen phrases. The induced hiding scheme can encode and decode arbitrary malicious instructions, thus revealing a new and subtle poisoning-induced vulnerability: covert control attacks.
  We precisely characterize covert control attacks and evaluate them across $5$ LLMs, $3$ backdoor defenses, and $4$ prompt injection defenses. With a small poisoned fraction, covert control attacks outperform heuristic-based prompt injection attacks in average attack success rate by about $40\%$ relative to clean fine-tuned models. They also circumvent defenses based on detection and fine-tuning, maintaining up to $93\%$ attack success rate after backdoor defenses and up to $98\%$ after prompt injection defenses.

</details>


### [103] [Control Physiology: An Agent-Based Model of FAIR-CAM Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26597)
*Jack Jones,Laura Voicu*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出了首个基于智能体的模型来操作化FAIR-CAM框架，通过医院勒索软件场景模拟揭示了静态分析无法捕捉的三种涌现动态：操作效能与理论公式之间约17%的偏差、修复管道中的排队机制转变导致损失放大2.8倍、以及监控失效在VMC拓扑中的级联传播。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: FAIR-CAM框架提供了控制动态的理论基础但一直停留在理论层面，需要将其操作化为可计算模型以揭示安全控制在实际运行中的动态行为特征。

Method: 构建了首个基于智能体的仿真模型，包含八种智能体类型、乘法纵深防御易感性公式、三源方差模型、预算约束修复机制，以及可为每个损失事件生成完整因果链的叙事因果引擎。

Result: 在医院勒索软件场景下（N=1000次迭代），发现了三种涌现动态：操作效能与FAIR-CAM公式存在约17%的偏差；修复预算低于阈值时排队机制转变导致损失放大2.8倍；监控失效在VMC拓扑中级联传播。这些动态是FAIR-CAM架构的结构性属性。

Conclusion: 该模型成功将FAIR-CAM框架从理论转化为可计算的仿真工具，揭示了静态分析无法捕捉的关键动态特征，且这些动态应具有跨场景的泛化能力。

Abstract: Security risk analysis typically treats control effectiveness as a static input, yet controls degrade through configuration drift, depend on monitoring systems that may themselves be degraded, and compete for finite remediation budgets. The FAIR Controls Analytics Model (FAIR-CAM) provides the theoretical framework for these dynamics but has so far remained theoretical. We present the first agent-based model to operationalize the core FAIR-CAM dynamics, making control physiology computationally observable, and release the implementation as open source. The simulation implements eight agent types, a multiplicative defense-in-depth susceptibility formula, a three-source variance model, budget-constrained remediation, and a narrative causation engine that produces a complete causal trace for every loss event. In a hospital ransomware scenario (N=1,000 iterations), three organizational dynamics emerge that static analysis cannot represent. First, emergent operational efficacy diverges from the analytical FAIR-CAM formula by approximately 17 percent, driven by correlated extrinsic variance; the divergence grows linearly with extrinsic frequency and vanishes under purely intrinsic drift. Second, a sharp queueing regime transition in the remediation pipeline approximately 2.8x expected loss when budget falls below a scenario-specific threshold (5-10 engineer-hours/month). Third, cascading monitoring failures propagate through the VMC topology: a single degraded VMC silently compounds undetected variance across the controls it manages. These dynamics are structural properties of the FAIR-CAM architecture and should generalize beyond the specific scenario studied.

</details>


### [104] [Batch Me If You Can: Coverage-guided RPKI Fuzzing at Scale](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26651)
*Haya Schulmann,Niklas Vogel*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为CAT的新型模糊测试工具，通过连续采样、函数侧信道覆盖率归因、带标签树的语义变异等技术，解决了RPKI多对象加密仓库的测试难题，相比现有工具实现66倍吞吐量提升和24-47%额外代码路径覆盖，发现了21个新漏洞（含8个CVE，CVSS 7.5-9.8），包括缓冲区溢出、DoS和仓库投毒逻辑漏洞。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RPKI协议对域间路由安全至关重要，但其软件缺乏充分测试。现有模糊测试工具（如AFL++、libFuzzer）假设单次执行对应单输入，无法处理RPKI仓库中数百个相互依赖、加密关联的对象，导致覆盖率反馈失效，难以发现深层漏洞。

Method: 提出三种关键技术：1) 连续采样（continuous sampling）实现多对象仓库的精准覆盖率归因；2) 利用函数作为侧信道进行每对象覆盖率归因；3) 将输入解析为带标签树，在保持密码有效性的前提下进行结构和语义变异。实现非顺序模糊测试与模板无关的ASN.1变异引擎。

Result: CAT相比顺序模糊测试实现66倍吞吐量提升；相比libFuzzer和先前工作探索多24-47%的独特代码路径；在RPKI验证器中发现21个未知漏洞，其中8个已分配CVE（CVSS评分7.5-9.8），涵盖缓冲区溢出、DoS和仓库投毒逻辑漏洞。

Conclusion: CAT有效解决了多对象加密关联协议（如RPKI）的模糊测试难题，证明了其方法的通用性，未来可扩展至DNSSEC、TLS等其他复杂密码协议。已开源以支持可复现性和后续研究。

Abstract: The Resource Public Key Infrastructure (RPKI) has become essential to secure inter-domain routing. Despite its critical role, RPKI software remains largely untested beyond shallow parsing. Existing fuzzers, like AFL++ or libFuzzer, do not work well for RPKI as they assume a single, self-contained input per execution, while RPKI repositories contain hundreds of interdependent cryptographically linked objects. Existing fuzzers fail to handle this complexity and lack the ability for precise coverage attribution in multi-object repositories, breaking feedback-based exploration and thereby missing most severe vulnerabilities in RPKI validation. In this paper, we overcome these limitations through novel fuzzing techniques, including continuous sampling and using functions as side-channels for per-object coverage attribution in large input repositories. We further show how parsing inputs to a labeled tree allows structural and semantic mutations while preserving cryptographic validity in mutated repositories. We implement our new techniques into a powerful fuzzing tool called CAT, combining non-sequential fuzzing with our template-agnostic ASN.1 mutation engine to achieve 66x throughput improvement over sequential fuzzing and exploring 24 - 47% more unique code paths compared to libFuzzer and previous work. Evaluating CAT on RPKI validators uncovered 21 previously unknown vulnerabilities with 8 CVEs already assigned (CVSS 7.5 - 9.8). These include a buffer overflow, Denial-of-Service (DoS), and exploitable repository-poisoning logic flaws. We open-source CAT to enable reproducibility, further research, and adaptation of our methods to other complex cryptography-based protocols such as DNSSEC and TLS.

</details>


### [105] [Resolving the Correct Library: A Loader-Level Defense Solution Against Shared Object Hijacking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26665)
*Can Ozkan,Dave Singelee*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于加载器的验证框架，通过路径绑定和Build-ID身份模型结合加密哈希，来强制保证动态链接器解析过程的真实性，从而防止Linux系统中的共享库劫持攻击。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有防御措施（如加固加载器、控制流完整性、文件完整性机制）均无法验证加载器实际解析的共享库是否是预期且可信的，存在关键空白。

Method: 提出加载器中心的验证框架，支持路径绑定和基于Build-ID的位置无关身份模型，结合加密哈希验证，在glibc上实现。

Result: 在通用Linux（Ubuntu）和嵌入式Linux（Buildroot）环境下的仿真评估表明，该机制能够有效防止共享库劫持攻击。

Conclusion: 共享库劫持本质上是加载器解析的真实性问题，所提框架通过强制验证解析结果的真实性填补了现有防御的空白。

Abstract: Shared library hijacking attacks in the Linux ecosystem, including embedded Linux, are a significant concern. It fundamentally exploits the dynamic linker's library-resolution semantics rather than modifying trusted libraries directly. Prior research has extensively analyzed attack vectors exploiting environment variables, embedded search paths, and dynamic loader internals, demonstrating that hijacking is rooted in fundamental loader behavior rather than isolated misconfigurations. Existing defenses either harden or replace the loader, enforce control-flow integrity after libraries are loaded, or apply file-centric integrity mechanisms such as signatures and measurement frameworks. However, these approaches fail to address a critical gap: none verify whether the shared object actually resolved by the loader is the intended and trusted one.
  In this paper, we argue that shared library hijacking is fundamentally a loader-resolution authenticity problem and present a loader-centric verification framework that enforces authenticity guarantees for the dynamic linker's resolution process. Our design supports both path-bound and location-independent (i.e., Build-ID-based) identity models combined with cryptographic hashing. We implement our approach on GNU libc (glibc) systems and evaluate it on both general-purpose Linux (e.g., Ubuntu) and embedded Linux (e.g., Buildroot) environments under emulation. Our results demonstrate that our proposed mechanism indeed prevents shared library hijacking attacks.

</details>


### [106] [Anonymous YARA Rules Are Not Anonymous](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26791)
*Usman Rabiu Isah,Laurent Bobelin,Pascal Berthomé*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: YARA规则共享存在可测量的OPSEC攻击面，仅移除元数据不足以保护贡献者身份。通过词法n-gram、语法AST特征和CodeBERT三种方法，对23,305条规则进行文体指纹分析，发现：仓库来源可99%准确识别，个体作者识别率达76%，恶意软件家族分类达95%。即使控制恶意软件家族，作者重识别平均准确率仍达74.6%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 挑战安全社区中"仅移除元数据即可保护贡献组织身份"的普遍假设，系统验证YARA规则文本本身是否泄露作者身份信息。

Method: 基于23,305条YARA规则（来自三大公共仓库），沿四个文体指纹维度（个体作者、来源仓库、恶意软件家族、时间漂移）训练独立分类器，采用三种互补方法：词法n-gram（Burrows' Delta）、语法AST特征（Caliskan-Islam）和微调CodeBERT。

Result: 仓库来源识别准确率达99%，个体作者重识别76%（显著高于随机水平），恶意软件家族分类95%。时间漂移导致仓库归因准确率下降9-18%。控制恶意软件家族后，7个家族中有5个的作者重识别平均准确率达74.6%。

Conclusion: 首次系统证明YARA规则共享构成可测量的OPSEC攻击面，仅移除元数据不足以缓解此风险。

Abstract: YARA rules are widely shared across threat intelligence communities to enable collective defence against malware. This practice implicitly assumes that removing metadata (e.g., author fields) sufficiently protects the identity of contributing organisations. To assess the validity of this assumption, we systematically evaluate how much can be inferred from YARA rule text alone. Specifically, using a corpus of 23,305 rules from three major public repositories, we train independent classifiers along four stylometric fingerprint dimensions: individual author, source repository, malware family, and temporal drift, using three complementary methods: lexical n-grams (Burrows' Delta), syntactic AST features (Caliskan-Islam), and fine-tuned CodeBERT. Our results demonstrate that repository origin is almost perfectly recoverable (up to 99% accuracy), individual authors can be re-identified well above chance (76%), and malware family classification reaches 95%. Comparing the same repository attribution task across full-history and time-restricted subsets reveals a 9-18% accuracy gap, providing preliminary evidence of temporal drift in repository fingerprints.To further disentangle content from style, we conduct per-malware family author attribution experiments. Even when the malware family is the same for all samples considered, authors can still be re-identified for five of seven tested families (mean accuracy 74.6%).
  These findings constitute the first systematic demonstration that YARA rule sharing is a measurable OPSEC attack surface, and that metadata removal alone does not mitigate it.

</details>


### [107] [Secure UAV Swarms in Low-Altitude Wireless Networks: Challenges and Solutions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26876)
*Yuntao Wang,Haojia Yang,Han Liu,Jianle Ba,Zhou Su*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文针对无人机蜂群面临的安全威胁（GPS欺骗、内部威胁、多跳入侵），提出了一种云-边-端协同防御框架，包含三种互补机制：基于博弈模型的抗GPS欺骗协同感知方案、基于行为驱动信任评估的身份认证方法，以及多智能体攻击取证框架。实验验证了方法的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无人机蜂群在低空应用中面临严重的开放性安全风险（GPS欺骗、内部威胁、多跳入侵），且受限于机载资源有限、拓扑频繁变化以及存在智能对抗者，现有防御手段难以有效应对。

Method: 1) 提出云-边-端协同防御框架；2) 基于交互式攻防博弈建模的协同感知方案抵抗GPS欺骗；3) 行为驱动的信任评估身份认证方法缓解内部威胁；4) 多智能体攻击取证框架追溯多跳攻击传播路径。

Result: 实验结果验证了所提出三种机制的有效性。

Conclusion: 本文构建的协同防御框架可有效应对无人机蜂群的多类安全威胁，并指出了若干开放式研究方向。

Abstract: Unmanned aerial vehicle (UAV) swarms are increasingly deployed in vast low-altitude applications, owing to their capabilities in distributed sensing, flexible communication, and autonomous coordination. Nevertheless, the open and highly dynamic operating environment of UAV swarms introduces serious security risks, including GPS spoofing, insider threats, and multi-hop intrusion. These threats are aggravated by limited on-board resources, frequently changing network topology, and the presence of intelligent adversaries. To tackle these issues, this paper proposes a cloud-edge-end collaborative defense framework for UAV swarms. Based on this framework, three complementary mechanisms are developed. First, a cooperative perception scheme is designed to resist GPS spoofing via interactive attack-defense game modeling. Second, a behavior-driven authentication method with trust evaluation is developed to mitigate insider threats. Third, a multi-agent attack forensics framework is devised to intelligently trace the propagation paths of multi-hop attacks in UAV networks. Experimental results validate the effectiveness of the proposed approaches. Finally, several open research directions are outlined.

</details>


### [108] [Privacy-Preserving Screening for Record Linkage](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26882)
*Chenyu Huang,Fan Zhang,Huangxun Chen,Yongjun Zhao,Huaming Rao,Peng Chen,Danqing Huang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出 Screening-then-Linkage 框架，通过引入轻量级筛选阶段（PPRS）解决两方隐私保护记录链接（PPRL）在大规模数据市场中的可扩展性问题。基于电路-PSI实现的 Appraisal 系统，提出 Oblivious Attribute/Feature Alignment 协议，支持近似匹配和模式感知设置，相比传统方案通信成本降低14倍，在相同约束下可处理记录数比SOTA系统SFour多850倍，比SOTA PPRL快165倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大数据和机器学习时代需要有价值的数据协作，但必须遵守监管和法律约束。两方隐私保护记录链接（PPRL）可评估潜在协作价值并保障隐私安全，但其高昂的计算和通信开销阻碍了在拥有大量潜在合作者的数据市场中的实际应用。

Method: 提出 Screening-then-Linkage 框架，在资源密集型PPRL阶段之前引入轻量级筛选阶段（PPRS）。基于电路-PSI构建 Appraisal 系统，并设计更高效的 Oblivious Attribute/Feature Alignment 协议（ obliviously 属性/特征对齐协议），解决电路-PSI仅支持对称函数的限制，支持更广泛的比较函数并提高效率。

Result: Appraisal在相同约束下可处理比SOTA PPRS系统SFour多850倍的记录，比SOTA PPRL快165倍；与常规协议相比，通信成本降低14倍。

Conclusion: 严格分析和全面实验评估证明了Appraisal的安全性、有效性和效率。Screening-then-Linkage框架显著减少了从大量候选者中识别最有价值合作者所需的计算时间。

Abstract: In an era dominated by big data and machine learning, establishing valuable data collaboration has never been more critical. However, such collaborations must operate under regulatory and legal constraints. Two-party Privacy-Preserving Record Linkage (PPRL) emerges to assess the potential collaboration value and also ensure the privacy and security of the involved data. Nevertheless, the substantial computational and communication overheads associated with PPRL hinder its practical adoption in data markets with numerous potential collaborators. Therefore, we present the Screening-then-Linkage framework, which incorporates a lightweight Screening phase prior to the resource-intensive PPRL phase, i.e., PPRS, to mitigate the scalability issue of PPRL. We propose a circuit-PSI-based system, named Appraisal to realize a secure, effective, and efficient PPRS. To reconcile the approximate matching and/or schema-aware setting required in PPRS with the limitations of the circuit-PSI supporting only symmetric functions, we propose a more communication-efficient secure permutation, i.e., Oblivious Attribute/Feature Alignment protocol tailored for PPRS. This protocol supports a broader range of comparison functions and significantly improves efficiency, i.e., reducing communication costs by a factor of 14 compared to the conventional protocol. Our rigorous analysis and comprehensive empirical evaluations demonstrate the security, effectiveness, and efficiency of Appraisal. Appraisal can accommodate up to $850\times$ more records than the SOTA PPRS system, SFour, within the same constraints. Moreover, it is $165 \times$ faster than SOTA PPRL, indicating the Screening-then-Linkage framework substantially decreases the computation time required to identify the most valuable collaborators from a large pool of candidates.

</details>


### [109] [The Fault in Our Drafts: Vulnerabilities in RPKI Specification and Software](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26986)
*Oliver Jacobsen,Tobias Kirsch,Haya Schulmann,Niklas Vogel,Michael Waidner*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文首次系统分析了RPKI RFC规范缺陷与实现漏洞及实际部署故障之间的因果关系，通过差异模糊测试、互联网范围爬取和验证日志分析，发现61个验证行为不一致、23个直接源于RFC缺陷、2个新漏洞获得CVE，并提出改进建议和实时告警服务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RPKI作为保障互联网路由安全的关键基础设施，其规范分散在数十个RFC中，存在模糊、冲突或未明确定义的要求，导致不同实现出现不一致行为和运营故障，但目前缺乏对这些规范缺陷与其实际影响之间因果关系的系统性研究。

Method: 结合差异模糊测试（针对主要RPKI实现）、互联网范围爬取和验证日志分析，将实际漏洞追溯回有缺陷的RFC需求，进行大规模、影响驱动的规范评估。

Result: 发现61个先前未记录的验证行为不一致，其中23个直接追溯到RFC缺陷；识别出2个新漏洞并获得CVE；揭示了这些不是孤立的编码错误，而是RPKI标准编写、解释和实现方式中的系统性问题。

Conclusion: 提出具体改进建议并引入新型告警服务，用于监控和报告RPKI部署中的实时不一致问题；开源数据集、代码和工具以支持可重复性和后续研究。

Abstract: The Resource Public Key Infrastructure (RPKI) secures the Internet's routing system by defining a complex trust and validation framework for certificates, Route Origin Authorizations (ROAs), manifests, and Certificate Revocation Lists (CRLs). These mechanisms are specified across dozens of RFCs. This paper presents the first comprehensive analysis of the causal link between flaws in RPKI Requests for Comments (RFCs) and vulnerabilities in implementations and real-world deployments. We reveal how vague, conflicting, or underspecified requirements in 50 RPKI RFCs propagate into inconsistent implementation behavior and operational failures. We conduct the first large-scale, impact-driven evaluation of RPKI specifications. Our methodology combines differential fuzzing of major RPKI implementations with Internet-wide crawling and validation log analysis, enabling us to trace practical vulnerabilities back to flawed RFC requirements. We uncover 61 previously undocumented inconsistencies in validation behavior, trace 23 directly to RFC flaws, and identify two novel vulnerabilities that were assigned CVEs. Our findings reveal that these are not isolated coding errors but rather systemic issues inherent in how RPKI standards are written, interpreted, and implemented. To mitigate these threats, we propose concrete recommendations and introduce a novel alerting service that monitors and reports live inconsistencies in RPKI deployments. Our open-source datasets, code, and tools support reproducibility and further research.

</details>


### [110] [BAIT: Boundary-Guided Disclosure Escalation via Self-Conditioned Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27110)
*Xuan Luo,Yue Wang,Geng Tu,Jing Li,Ruifeng Xu*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: BAIT是一种三步式越狱框架，通过让模型自我识别、完善保护边界并最终要求详细示例，利用模型自身的推理和一致性倾向逐步泄露有害内容，在多个基准测试中取得高攻击成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有大语言模型越狱方法效果有限，需要一种更有效的攻击框架来揭示模型安全机制的漏洞。

Method: 提出BAIT框架，包含三步：1）要求模型识别保护边界；2）要求模型完善该边界；3）要求提供详细示例。每一步基于模型前一步的回复展开，利用模型的推理和一致性倾向。

Result: 在AdvBench、JailbreakBench、AIR-Bench和SORRY-Bench上，BAIT在多个顶级大语言模型上持续实现高攻击成功率，显著超越传统越狱基线。

Conclusion: 1）预防导向的框架显著优于直接知识请求；2）完善步骤在泄露升级中起关键作用；3）前两步有一定概率引发有害内容且触发过滤较少。

Abstract: In this work, we propose BAIT (Boundary-Aware Iterative Trap), a three-step jailbreak framework that approaches malicious goals through internal disclosure. BAIT first asks the model to identify the protection boundary, then requires it to refine that boundary, and finally requests a detailed example.
